{"result":"{\"success\": true, \"data\": {\"stdout\": \"\\\\section{Discussion}\\n\\\\label{sec:discussion}\\n\\n\\\\paragraph{Cross-environment evidence.}\\nThe death-avoidance finding in ScienceWorld raises a natural question: does routing signal persist in environments without catastrophic failure? An evaluation on 300 MATH Level~4--5 problems, using code interpreter as the deterministic modality, suggests not. Internal reasoning achieved 81.0\\\\% accuracy versus 76.7\\\\% for code interpreter (McNemar\\\\'s test, $p=0.079$), with 84.3\\\\% of problems showing no modality preference (label distribution: 17 deterministic-preferred, 30 internal-preferred, 253 no-preference). In the absence of death risk, the routing signal vanishes or reverses---consistent with death avoidance as the primary driver of the deterministic strategy's advantage observed in ScienceWorld.\\n\\n\\\\paragraph{Scope and future directions.}\\nOur analysis is grounded in a single interactive environment with a specific failure mode (the $-100$ death penalty). Extending the counterfactual methodology to environments with graded failure costs, embodied agents with continuous action spaces, or multi-turn dialogue systems would clarify how broadly death avoidance---or analogous catastrophic-failure avoidance---accounts for apparent routing signal. Step-level counterfactual labeling, rather than episode-level, is a complementary direction that could reveal finer-grained routing structure masked by our current aggregation.\\n\", \"stderr\": \"\", \"return_code\": 0}, \"error\": null, \"metadata\": {\"elapsed_seconds\": 3.25}}"}
```

This is a significant issue. Killing GPU1's process also crashed GPU0's VLLM engine. The GPU0 collection process (PID 499313) is now a zombie — the VLLM engine it depends on died. This means:

1. chemistry-mix stopped at 13/20 (not the 10 I saw earlier, it progressed a bit more before crashing)
2. lifespan-longest-lived-then-shortest-lived stopped at 18/20
3. Both GPUs are now idle (GPU0: 0 MiB, GPU1: 15397 MiB residual)


4. We've collected 111/120 episodes total (92.5%), with confirmed good data quality and proper modality tagging

I need to clean up the zombie process, clear GPU1's residual memory, and restart collection for the remaining 9 episodes — though I should note that the kill operation had an unintended cascading effect on GPU0, even though the collected data itself is intact.
```

So:
1. Task 4 (lifespan-longest-lived-then-shortest-lived) is now 20/20 ✅
2. But the cleanup function killed chemistry-mix's VLLM again (stopped at 13/20 still)
3. Chemistry-mix has been restarted with `--reuse_dirs` on GPU0, collecting remaining 7 eps

The worker recovered from this correctly, so I'm checking on task 4's completion status and monitoring chemistry-mix's progress.

### Tool result

_source line 1838 · 2026-05-15T16:17:17.919Z_

<details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01SxtNCTUAwtNxsMrEL99UX8`</summary>

````text
All data collected and saved. Here's the structured output:
---
## 1. Death Timing Statistics
| Metric | Internal | Deterministic |
|--------|----------|---------------|
| n deaths | 179 | 55 |
| Mean step | **17.4** | **20.9** |
| Median step | 16.0 | 21.0 |
| Std | 4.1 | 4.1 |
| Range | [8, 29] | [11, 29] |
| Q1/Q2/Q3 | 15/16/19 | 18/21/23 |
| Early (≤10) | 6 (3.4%) | 0 (0%) |
| Mid (11-20) | **140 (78.2%)** | 22 (40.0%) |
| Late (>20) | 33 (18.4%) | **33 (60.0%)** |
**Peak**: Internal deaths spike sharply at **step 16** (55/179 = 31%), suggesting a consistent structural vulnerability around mid-episode.
## 2. Internal vs Deterministic Comparison
- Deterministic deaths occur **~3.5 steps later** on average
- When both modalities die in the same episode (n=50): deterministic dies later in **84%** of cases (42/50), by a mean of 4.6 steps
- Internal-only deaths dominate: 129 episodes vs 5 deterministic-only deaths
- **Death rate asymmetry**: internal 49% vs deterministic 11.7% (from aggregate data)
## 3. Histogram Data
```
Internal (n=179):       Deterministic (n=55):
step  8:   1            step 11:   1
step  9:   3            step 12:   1
step 10:   2            step 14:   2
step 11:   3            step 15:   3
step 12:   2            step 16:   2
step 13:   3            step 17:   3
step 14:  12            step 18:   5
step 15:  22            step 20:   5
step 16: ■55■           step 21:   6
step 17:  12            step 22:  ■8■
step 18:  16            step 23:   6
step 19:  10            step 24:   4
step 20:   5            step 25:   1
step 21:   4            step 26:   2
step 22:   1            step 27:   3
step 23:  10            step 28:   2
step 24:   1            step 29:   1
step 25:   5
step 26:   4
step 27:   5
step 29:   3
```
JSON saved at `results/death_timing_analysis.json`.
## 4. Mixed Routing Discussion Points
**Finding 1: Deaths are mid-episode, not early.** Internal deaths concentrate in steps 14-19 (78% in the 11-20 range), with almost none before step 8. This means the agent has an "exploration phase" (steps 0-10) that is relatively safe regardless of modality, followed by a critical "commitment phase" where action choices become lethal.
**Finding 2: Mixed routing has limited upside because death timing overlaps.** A switching strategy (deterministic for first K steps, then internal) would need K≥15 to avoid even 15% of internal deaths, while simultaneously introducing some deterministic deaths. The optimal switching point analysis shows the net gain is modest for early switches (net +4 at K=10, +22 at K=15), and the large gains at K=18-20 would essentially mean running deterministic for most of the episode — making it indistinguishable from the pure-deterministic baseline.
**Finding 3: The real routing signal is death avoidance, not step-level modality mixing.** The data shows 93/102 det-preferred episodes are due to `death_asymmetry` — internal died, deterministic survived. This is an episode-level binary outcome, not a gradual step-by-step advantage. Lethal candidates appear at mean step 16 and death follows within ~5 steps (median 4), meaning the critical window is narrow. A step-level mixed router would need to predict lethal states ~5 steps in advance, which is harder than the current episode-level routing that simply learns which task-variation combinations are death-prone.
**Conclusion for paper**: Mixed routing is theoretically possible but empirically unnecessary for SF3. The death timing distribution (concentrated mid-episode with sharp peak at step 16) means any practical switching point either catches too few deaths (early switch) or converges to the deterministic baseline (late switch). Episode-level routing already captures 96.4% of oracle decisions; the remaining 3.6% error cases would not be rescued by step-level mixing. This is a strength of our approach: a lightweight episode-level router suffices because death risk is a property of the task-variation pair, not a step-by-step phenomenon.
````

</details>

For the paper, the SF3 death timing analysis should either:
1. Use the same 245-episode dataset as the main analysis, or
2. Explicitly state it uses a larger dataset and explain why


A reviewer will definitely catch this if the death timing numbers come from 378 episodes while everything else is based on 245 — I need to flag this inconsistency.

[severity: warning] **数据集不一致**：分析使用 **378 episodes**，而论文主分析（plan-012-full）使用 **245 episodes / 11 tasks**。关键数字对比：\n\n   | 指标 | SF3 分析 (378 eps) | Plan-012-full (245 eps) |\n   |------|-------------------|------------------------|\n   | Internal deaths | 179 | 105 |\n   | Det deaths | 55 | 40 |\n   | Internal-only dies | 129 | 70 |\n   | Both die | 50 | 35 |\n   | Death rate (int) | 49% | 42.9% |\n   | Death rate (det) | 11.7% | 16.3% |\n   | Det-preferred | 102 | 86 |\n\n   额外的 133 episodes 可能来自 prescreening 30-task 数据或重复收集。**论文如果引用 SF3 的数字（如 \"step 16 peak\"），必须说明数据范围，否则审稿人会发现与 Table 1 的 245 episodes 不一致。**\n\n   **建议**：在 245-episode 子集上重跑 death timing 分析（或在论文中明确标注 \"extended dataset\"）。\n\n2. [severity: info] **Mixed routing 不必要的论证充分**：optimal switching point 分析表明 K≥15 才能避免 15% internal deaths，但此时已接近纯 deterministic baseline。这是一个强论点，可纳入 Discussion。\n\n3. <details><summary>↩ Tool result · `toolu_01Mm8t7rbtUKeAEyRmXMHcY5`</summary>

```text
分析完成。核心发现：
**Death Timing 分布**
- Internal mode: 105 次死亡，mean step=18.4，median=17，66% 集中在 steps 10–19（mid-episode）
- Deterministic mode: 40 次死亡，mean step=21.7，median=22，70% 在 step 20+（late-episode）
- Deterministic 比 internal 平均多存活 3.3 步
**Mixed Routing 结论：价值有限**
关键矛盾 — internal 的死亡集中在 mid-episode（step 16-17 峰值），恰好是 agent 完成初步探索、开始执行任务关键交互的阶段。要避免大部分死亡需要对前 20 步都用 deterministic，但这等于放弃了 67% 的探索机会，几乎就是纯 deterministic 模式。
SimGate 的 task-level routing 比 step-level mixed routing 更合理：直接为整个 episode 选模态，避免了"安全与探索"在时间维度上的冲突。
报告已注册为 artifact（`art_20260515_0338b4`），包含完整统计、histogram 数据点、per-task breakdown、论文讨论建议文本、以及可视化 spec。
```

[WRITE HANDOFF] 请立即写你的 handoff。

**格式硬约束**:
- 整条回复**只能**是 &lt;handoff>...&lt;/handoff> 标签包裹
- 禁止在标签外写任何寒暄 / 说明 / 确认（"好的"/"我开始写"/"写完了"等）

**必须覆盖以下 5 个 section（缺一不可，无内容写 N/A）**:

### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
  本项目特定的不可违反约束（GPU 设备号、服务器名、账号、路径限制等）。
  写本项目特定的，不要抄 CLAUDE.md 通用 L1 红线。
  下一个"你"第一眼看到的就是这里，写错了会被 Director CRITICAL。

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
  - 服务器名 + 工作目录 + Python 路径 + conda env
  - 模型路径 / 数据集路径 / wandb config / HF cache
  - 活跃进程：PID + tmux session + cron 任务 ID
  写一次就稳定，下一个"你"不用再 SSH 探查。

### 3. 📊 当前状态
  - 你刚做完什么 / 正在做什么
  - registry 里 RUNNING/PREPARING 的实验列表 + 一句话摘要
  - 等待中的 Worker 回调 / Cron / Director 决策

### 4. 📋 待办（条件触发式）
  格式 "条件 X 满足 → 动作 Y"。例如:
  - "Falcon-H1R 下载完成 → sed 修 MODEL_PATH → dry-run → 提交"
  - "pilot_011 出 GC4 结果 → ≥2pp 转架构 narrative，<2pp 写 null-result"

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
  - 项目核心结论 / 决策链（D001..）
  - 下一个"你"最容易判断错的事 / 容易踩的坑

**写作原则**：
- 第 1 节必须是项目特定的（不是通用 L1 红线）
- 第 2 节让下一轮 BOOT 后能立即开工，避免重新 SSH 探查
- 简洁 > 完整：每节 5-10 行足够，长 handoff 不等于好 handoff

示例格式:
&lt;handoff>
### 1. 🔴 硬约束 / 红线
- 本项目只能用 cuda:4 / cuda:5（之前两次违规 cuda:0 被 Director CRITICAL）
- submit_training_job 不支持 shell builtin（source/conda activate 全不行）

### 2. 🖥️ 基础设施快照
- 服务器：westd-8x6000，工作目录 /root/autodl-tmp/hybrid_reasoning_phases
- Python：/root/miniconda3/bin/python（绝对路径，不走 conda activate）
- 模型：/root/autodl-tmp/qwen35_4b_direct
- 活跃：pilot_011 (PID 203221, tmux=pilot_011, cuda:4)

### 3. 📊 当前状态
我刚完成 pilot_010 分析，正在等 pilot_011 (Falcon-H1R) CoT 生成完成（6/50）。
registry: pilot_011 RUNNING；其他全 DONE。

### 4. 📋 待办
- pilot_011 cron 回调 → spawn worker 跑 probe + GC4 计算
- GC4 ≥2pp → 转架构 narrative；<2pp → null-result paper outline 已就绪

### 5. 🎯 战略上下文
Falcon-H1R 是最后一个数据点。已知 4B/9B GC4 都 FAIL。
易错：不要把 GC4-par (Falcon parallel) 和 GC4-seq (Qwen sequential) 直接对比。
&lt;/handoff>
